2026.11.19 早报

覆盖窗口：2026-11-18T06:00:00+08:00 → 2026-11-19T06:00:00+08:00 · 更新时间：2026.11.19 06:46 北京时间 · 639 条

窗口：2026-11-18 06:00 至 2026-11-19 06:00（北京时间，UTC+8）｜监控名单 134 个账号｜所有时间均为北京时间。

Executive Summary｜核心结论

本期窗口为北京时间 2026-11-18 06:00 至 2026-11-19 06:00（UTC 2026-11-17T22:00:00Z 含，至 2026-11-18T22:00:00Z 不含），134 个监控账号全部分页至耗尽。与 11-18 晚报相比，夜盘到今早新落地的主线是：多名名单内账号称 Anthropic IPO 定价为每股 83 美元；Epoch 援引 S-1 给出其算力开支结构；腾讯称现金 capex 将从 2028 年起回落；SemiAnalysis 记录日本 neocloud Datasection 全年收入指引下调 89%。窗口前半段仍包含 NVIDIA Q3 FY27 财报与存储“锁量”证据，需要放在同一 24 小时里一起看。以下【分析】是本摘要的判断；“据称/作者认为”是发帖人的说法，未经独立核实；名单外关键词条目另有标注。

Anthropic IPO｜转述定价 83 美元，S-1 显示烧的主要是训练

定价：@jukan05（n=1034）称 “ANTHROPIC PRICES IPO AT $83 PER SHARE”；@Loris_Dalleau（n=1039）在同一时点转述每股 83 美元、估值 1.5 万亿美元。这是名单内对定价落地的转述，不是本摘要核对过的公司公告或最终招股文件；估值数字来自转述而非 @jukan05 原文。名单外快讯还称 Cboe 计划最早周一为 $ANT 挂出期权，并同样转述 83 美元 / 约 1.5 万亿美元（k4）。

开支结构（Epoch 援引 S-1，相对更可核对）：@EpochAIResearch（n=1022）称 Anthropic 仅 2026 年第三季度就在算力和基础设施上花费 100 亿美元，年化 400 亿美元，对比 2023 年全年 4.33 亿美元，开支增速约 4 倍/年，快于 OpenAI 的约 3 倍/年。同线程（n=1024）称 2026 年前九个月客户推理只占其算力开支约 26%，低于 2025 年的 38%，大部分用于研发和其他内部用途。@AndrewCurran_（n=1053）另转述“上季约一半花在 SpaceX”，这是二手说法，未在 Epoch 原帖中出现，本摘要不把它当成已核实事实。

对 OpenAI / 持股方的映射：@pennykoi（n=840）把修订 S-1 的收入口径与业内传闻放在一起，称 10 月底 run-rate 约 810 亿美元、仅 10 月净增约 100 亿美元，并认为 OpenAI ARR 约 500 亿美元出头、空头在把明年亏损放在 700–800 亿美元——后半段是作者观点和传闻，不是文件原文。@RihardJarc（n=680）认为 $ANT 上市后，Amazon 持有的股份几天或几周内会超过 $AMZN 当前市值的 20%，这是作者判断。@aleabitoreddit（n=858）把 Anthropic“最终采购协议”加总到约 9,009 亿美元并拆到 AVGO/Google/SpaceX/Amazon 等对手方，属于作者汇编，本摘要未核对合同。

【分析】如果 83 美元和约 1.5 万亿美元估值的转述最终被文件确认，市场是在给“高增长 + 高烧钱”同时定价。Epoch 引用的 S-1 数字更关键：年化 400 亿美元算力开支、且大部分不是客户推理，意味着上市后首先被检验的是训练/研发投入能否继续转成增量 ARR，而不是当前推理毛利率看起来有多瘦。对 $AMZN 的持股重估是合理的思考框架，但 20% 市值的具体比例取决于持股数量、锁定期和上市后成交价，不能把作者预测写成事实。

NVIDIA｜需求数字仍强，折价来自治理、对华停运和口径

财报数字（多名名单内账号引述电话会，口径一致）：Q3 收入 1,158 亿美元，同比 +103%，Bloomberg 一致预期约 1,090 亿；数据中心 1,076 亿美元；Non-GAAP 毛利率 74.9%、EPS 2.68 美元；Q4 指引 1,300 亿美元 ±3%；FY28 增速展望从约 70% 上调到约 75%；云 backlog 从 2 万亿升至 2.5 万亿美元；新增 1,500 亿美元回购，剩余授权约 2,273 亿美元（@sssjeffpu n=339 等）。

对华与治理的新细节：@kimmonismus（n=649，转述路透）引 Kress 原话称不再向中国出货已获许可的 Hopper 数据中心产品，上季度这类产品在中国销售额 24 亿美元，当前指引已不含此项。@jukan05（n=889）转述彭博：董事会已接触 Arm CEO Rene Haas，探讨由其出任下一任 CEO——这是媒体报道，不是公司确认。夜盘仍在传播董事会拒绝黄仁勋复职、以及发放现金/提前加薪留人的报道（@AndrewCurran_ n=748、n=873；@kimmonismus n=948）。

需求口径需要打折：@RihardJarc（n=648）指出，NVIDIA 在 FY26 Q2 把 $SPCX 从 ACIE 划到 Hyperscaler 分部；按 SpaceX capex 反推，作者估计 SPCX 在 Q3 可能占 NVIDIA 支出 50–100 亿美元，因此“原超大规模云客户”（能用经营现金流覆盖大部分开支的那些）近几季可能只是略增甚至持平。这是作者对分部调整的还原，不是公司拆分。

【分析】收入、指引和 backlog 仍在往上走，但 24 亿美元中国 Hopper 已从指引中归零，说明对华收入应按接近零建模，而不是按“还能出许可芯片”来外推。市场把股价压在 52 周低位附近（@Omercheema n=704 的观察），主要折价来自治理、中国和 2028 可见度，而不是本季需求数字本身。Rene Haas 接触若属实，是关键人物风险的一个潜在出口，但也引入 Arm/SoftBank 一侧的利益冲突问题；在公司确认前只能当作传闻。hyperscaler 需求斜率要用分部调整后的口径看，否则容易把 SpaceX 的并表贡献当成传统云厂的加速。

存储｜Micron 称 2027 已售出 75%，DRAM 与 NAND 继续分化

最硬的公司侧新证据：@BenBajarin（n=868）引用 Micron CEO Sanjay Mehrotra 的说法，需求仍强、供给持续紧张，2027 和 2028 年都会更紧，2027 年已售出 75%。名单外多条笔记对同一场交流作了补充：26 份 SCA（供应承诺协议）多为五年期、公司称不会像过去的 LTA 那样被撕毁，堆叠 3D DRAM 展望在 2028–2029 年，股东回报计划 12 月 9 日起步（k1–k3；部分原文在“Compute”处截断，未补全）。@SixSigmaCapital（n=741）转述 Lip-Bu Tan 称数据中心需求强、存储厂商 80% 营业利润率“惊人”，并看到 2027 和 2028 年 capex 更高。

窗口前半段仍在的锁量证据：NVIDIA 供应承诺两个季度内从 1,190 亿升至 2,790 亿再至 3,900 亿美元，主要是多年期存储采购（@TradexWhisperer n=274）。@jukan05（n=369）转述 TrendForce：2026 Q4 常规 DRAM ASP 环比 +10–15%，2027 年涨幅逐季放缓；HBM4 价格 2027 年同比或超 100%；NAND 因长江存储扩产，预计 2027 Q2 起下跌。@dnystedt（n=222）转述媒体：长鑫存储 Q3 收入同比 +613.2% 至 1,187.1 亿元，前九个月毛利率 86.85%，称 DDR5 短缺持续到 Q4。

【分析】Micron“2027 已售出 75%、2027–2028 更紧”与 NVIDIA 3,900 亿美元存储采购是同一方向的证据，对 $MU / SK hynix / 三星仍然有利；但 80% 营业利润率是当下景气的结果，不是可以外推到 2028 的保证。TrendForce 的分化仍然成立：DRAM/HBM 的可见度强于 NAND。长鑫的利润率和扩产意愿，是 2027 年后 DRAM 定价的主要下行变量。名单外 SCA 细节（26 份、五年期、不易违约）若被公司材料确认，说明量的可见度在提高，但价格是否锁定、有没有上限，本窗口没有新的硬文件。

中国 capex 与 neocloud｜腾讯 2028 回落，Datasection 是压力测试

@jukan05（n=728）引用腾讯口径：AI 基建开支目前已接近峰值，现金 capex 在 2026 年余下时间和 2027 年可能波动，此后回落；管理层把这比作建工厂，并认为最新对华芯片限制会拖慢先进训练，但不实质损害由推理驱动、且可跑在国产芯片上的云增长。@FredaDuan（n=755）把刘炽平的逻辑概括为前置投资、2028 年硬件更便宜、效率提升，并认为行业年度 AI capex 若常态化到低于 1 万亿美元，再给正常化利润一个中档倍数，就是这个行业的总体量——后一句是作者框架，不是腾讯指引。

最具体的信用事件来自 @SemiAnalysis_ 线程：Datasection（3905.T）把全年收入指引下调 89%，从 1,620 亿日元降到 170 亿日元，股价跌停（n=901）。作者称这就是金融时报报道的“日本 neocloud”，中方承购、NVIDIA Blackwell、日本旗号；单一渠道 305 亿日元、占 FY3/26 收入 90.7%，FT 指客户为腾讯，在大阪租用约 1.5 万块 Blackwell、合同约 12 亿美元、为期三年（n=899）。11 月 16 日同一客户大额追加订单取消，原预期 799 亿日元收入变成 56 亿日元“安排和转介费”，净利指引由 +87 亿日元转为 -15 亿日元（n=904）。Datasection 未说明取消原因；SemiAnalysis 将其与对华远程访问规则挂钩，并称中方承购撤离后，这类 neocloud 不会是最后一家（n=906）。

【分析】腾讯“2028 年起现金 capex 回落”与 DRAM/HBM 锁量到 2027 并不立刻矛盾：它说的是开支斜率在 2028 年向下，而不是 2026–27 年需求消失。若这一口径被更多中国 CSP 跟从，风险首先打在 2028 年的 GPU 和存储斜率上，NAND 已经有 2027 Q2 转弱的预测，会更先感受到。Datasection 是更近的信用测试：无论最终原因是不是远程访问规则，90% 收入集中在单一中方渠道的 neocloud，在规则收紧时会同时砸在 GPU、租约和 ODM 账单上。这会强化 NVIDIA 只对“融资到位 + 有承购”的规模化 neocloud 做担保的逻辑，二线 GPU 云的融资条件和估值仍应给更高的信用折扣。

模型｜谷歌官方推出 Gemini 4 Neon，卖点是任务成本和速度

第一方：@sundarpichai（n=1066）宣布 Gemini 4 Neon 开始在 Gemini API / AI Studio、企业 Gemini、付费 Pro/Ultra 的 Antigravity 和 Gemini App 推送，并附与 Claude Opus 5.5、GPT-6.2 Sol 的基准对比（图中 Neon 在 AA Index 为 61，对比 59 和 57 等，部分终端/代码项 Opus 更高）。@demishassabis（n=1069）宣布已向 Pro / Ultra 用户开放。名单内评测：@chetaslua（n=1011）给出 AI Studio 定价，输入 3 美元 / 输出 15 美元每百万 tokens，1M 上下文，四档 thinking；@ArtificialAnlys（n=1097）称这是七周内第四款模型，Intelligence Index 61，相对 Carbon 成本约低 17%、耗时约低 40%；Agent 侧 Neon (xhigh) 每任务成本 6.86 美元，低于 Opus 的 12.61 美元和 Fable 的 15.47 美元（n=1096）。@TokenGremlin（n=1049）提醒：11 月 30 日前 Carbon 仍是 2/10 美元促销价，之后升至 4/20 美元，届时 Neon 的 3/15 美元对日常任务更便宜。

窗口前半段的 GPT-6.2 叙事没有被推翻：独立评测仍显示它在单价和效率上有竞争力，但编程和真实工作任务并未整体超越 Fable 5.2。@chetaslua（n=660）还提醒，有仿冒账号在传播“Gemini 4 Neon numbers”，头像被盗用，这类数字不能当第一方数据。

【分析】谷歌这次用官方渠道和可核对的单价把 Neon 推到前台，竞争点明确是“接近前沿、更便宜、更快”，而不是再讲一版无法核验的内部分数。对 Anthropic 上市前后的“能力领先”叙事，这增加了一个定价锚；对应用层和聚合层，token 单价和每任务成本继续下移是利好。促销期与正式价的切换（11 月 30 日）会影响短期用量迁移，不改变中期价格方向。

要点：Anthropic的算力开支每年增长4倍，快于OpenAI的3倍。根据其S-1，Anthropic仅在2026年第三季度就在算力和基础设施上花费了100亿美元，年化400亿美元，而2023年全年仅为4.33亿美元。（附图：年化算力开支曲线，Anthropic约4倍/年，90%置信区间3.6–4.5倍；OpenAI约3倍/年，并标出OpenAI的2026年预测。）

为何重要：Epoch 援引 S-1：2026Q3 算力与基础设施开支 100 亿美元、年化 400 亿美元，增速约 4 倍/年、快于 OpenAI 的约 3 倍。把 IPO 估值从口号拉回到可核对的烧钱规模。同线程 n=1024 补充：前九个月客户推理只占算力开支约 26%。

#3 ★@BenBajarin · 2026-11-19 02:23 北京时间

要点：只是时间问题，市场就会理解，已经有一批存储产品从大宗商品变成了价值型产品。理解这一点，你就会理解为什么利润率能维持在高于历史均值的水平。（引用Micron CEO Sanjay Mehrotra：Micron拥有6.2万项专利，是今天唯一在美国生产存储的公司；公司已完成转型，有良好位置继续受益于AI革命；需求依然强劲，供给持续紧张，2027和2028年都会紧；已经在和客户谈2028年，2027年已售出75%；2027会更强；算力性能的提升持续快于内存带宽。）

为何重要：名单内引用 Micron CEO：2027 年已售出 75%，2027 和 2028 年供给都会更紧。这是存储“锁量”叙事在公司管理层口径上的新证据，直接映射 $MU / SK hynix 的可见度。

原帖：https://x.com/BenBajarin/status/2123120031252754522 ｜ 跳至完整条目

#5 @jukan05 · 2026-11-18 23:56 北京时间

要点：哇……腾讯说预计现金capex将从2028年开始下降。 Lol。你觉得背后是什么？美国对华出口限制？（附图：公司称AI基建开支目前已接近峰值，现金CapEx在2026年余下时间和2027年可能波动，此后回落。管理层把这比作建工厂：现在投资，以便日后能以更低成本训练模型并生成token。腾讯还称云收入应继续受益于AI需求，尤其是可在国产芯片上运行的推理负载。管理层认为，最新的美国半导体限制将拖慢中国的先进训练，但不会实质损害由推理驱动的云增长。）

为何重要：腾讯称现金 capex 将从 2028 年起回落，并给出“开支接近峰值、推理可走国产芯片”的管理层口径。这是对 2028 年 GPU/存储斜率最清楚的中国 CSP 信号，需与 2026–27 年仍然紧张的存储锁量分开看。

#7 @TradexWhisperer · 2026-11-18 10:47 北京时间

要点：$NVDA 今天财报里最重要的一句话：

供应承诺在两个季度内从 $1190亿 → $2790亿 → $3900亿，原因是多年期的内存供应采购。$MU $SKHY

“我们与广泛的供应商网络合作，确保获得满足未来数年需求所需的关键组件。我们的供应承诺从上季度的 $2790亿 增加到 $3900亿，主要与多年期内存供应采购有关。”

看涨！

为何重要：把财报里最重要的资产负债表变化单独抽出：供应承诺两个季度内从 1,190 亿升至 3,900 亿美元，主要是多年期存储采购。这是 $MU / $SKHY 需求可见度的直接来源。

#10 @jukan05 · 2026-11-18 13:02 北京时间

要点：TrendForce：

由于中国 YMTC（长江存储）积极扩产，预计 NAND 价格将从 2027 年 Q2 开始下跌。相比之下，DRAM 价格预计将坚挺至 2027 年底。

预计 2026 年 Q4 常规 DRAM 平均售价（ASP）环比上涨 10–15%。TrendForce 预测 2027 年涨幅将更为平缓：Q1 ASP 环比上涨 5–10%，Q2 上涨 3–8%，Q3 和 Q4 均上涨 0–5%。

TrendForce 预计 HBM4 价格将从 2026 年的至少每 Gb 2.05 美元，上升到 2027 年的每 Gb 3.00–5.50 美元，预计同比涨幅超过 100%。下一代 HBM4E 的价格讨论在每 Gb 5 美元或更高。

为何重要：TrendForce 给出 DRAM / HBM / NAND 的分季价格路径：HBM4 2027 年同比或超 100%，NAND 预计 2027 Q2 起因长江存储扩产而下跌。仍是判断 $MU / SK hynix 与 $SNDK / 铠侠分化的最完整价格框架。

有些人看到这些数字会觉得超大规模云厂商对$NVDA的开支增长很快，尤其是上个季度；但必须考虑到，$NVDA在26年第二季度把$SPCX从“ACIE”分部划到了“Hyperscaler”分部。

结合$SPCX的capex数字，可以假设$SPCX在26年第三季度约占$NVDA支出的$50亿–$100亿，因此原来的超大规模分部（排除$SPCX后，也就是那些大部分能用经营现金流覆盖开支的公司）近几个季度其实只是略增，甚至可能持平。（附图：NVIDIA超大规模云收入与AI云/工业/企业收入季度对比柱状图）

原文：While some might look at these numbers and think the hyperscalers' spending on $NVDA is growing fast, especially in the last quarter, one has to take into account that $NVDA moved $SPCX in Q2 26 from the "ACIE" segment to the "Hyperscaler" segment.

Given $SPCX Capex numbers, one could assume that $SPCX was $5-$10B of $NVDA spend in Q3 26, so the original Hyperscale segment, excluding $SPCX (the companies that can actually finance most of this organically), is actually only slightly up to maybe flat in the last few quarters.

2026-11-19 00:18 北京时间 ｜ Source: @FredaDuan ｜ 原帖链接

$Tencent / 中国CSP对AI capex周期的口径非常清楚——

刘炽平给出了为什么AI capex应在2027年后放缓的明确理由：

1/ 前置投资：AI基础设施像建工厂。建成后可以持续训练新模型并生成token。

2/ 更低的硬件成本：同样的设备到2028年应该会便宜得多。

3/ 快速的效率提升：同一套基础设施随时间能提供指数级更多的训练算力和token产出。

启示：Martin实际上是在说AI capex将在2026-27年见顶，此后资本开支强度会明显下降，即使AI需求继续增长。

对半导体的读后感很有意思：如果行业年度AI capex最终常态化到低于$1万亿，再给正常化利润一个中档倍数，那大概就是这个行业的总体量。

原文：$Tencent / Chinese CSPs’ tone on the AI capex cycle is very clear -

Martin Lau laid out a clear case for why AI capex should moderate after 2027:

1/ Front-loaded investment: AI infrastructure is like building a factory. Once built, it can continuously train new models and generate tokens.

2/ Lower hardware costs: The same equipment should cost significantly less by 2028.

3/ Rapid efficiency gains: The same infrastructure can deliver exponentially more training compute and token output over time.

Takeaway: Martin is effectively calling for a peak in AI capex in 2026-27, with a meaningful decline in capex intensity thereafter, even as AI demand continues to grow.

The read-through for semis is interesting: If annual industry AI capex eventually normalizes below $1T, assigning a mid-range multiple to normalized earnings, that’s your industry total size.

“涨了就卖吧。”
“别买股票，买黄金。”

这些想必是出于担心我而给出的真心建议，但我又是个“变态”，会反复琢磨说这些话的人背后的心理。

结论是：那些我以为是为我好的建议，
其实是让提建议的人自己心里舒服的选择。
（因为对对方有好感，就会产生担心，而这是想要减轻这种担心的心理）

如果继续涨，反正已经止盈了，没关系；
如果跌了，就证明我说对了。
黄金涨了也好，
跌了也不至于破产。

很容易倾向于“不挨骂”而不是“赚钱”。

@jukan05（保留 23 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 30 条）

2026-11-18 13:02 北京时间 ｜ Source: @jukan05 ｜ 原帖链接

TrendForce：

由于中国 YMTC（长江存储）积极扩产，预计 NAND 价格将从 2027 年 Q2 开始下跌。相比之下，DRAM 价格预计将坚挺至 2027 年底。

预计 2026 年 Q4 常规 DRAM 平均售价（ASP）环比上涨 10–15%。TrendForce 预测 2027 年涨幅将更为平缓：Q1 ASP 环比上涨 5–10%，Q2 上涨 3–8%，Q3 和 Q4 均上涨 0–5%。

TrendForce 预计 HBM4 价格将从 2026 年的至少每 Gb 2.05 美元，上升到 2027 年的每 Gb 3.00–5.50 美元，预计同比涨幅超过 100%。下一代 HBM4E 的价格讨论在每 Gb 5 美元或更高。

原文：TrendForce:

NAND prices are expected to start falling in Q2 2027 as China’s YMTC aggressively expands production capacity. DRAM prices, by contrast, are expected to hold up through the end of 2027.

Conventional DRAM average selling prices (ASPs) are expected to rise 10–15% QoQ in Q4 2026. TrendForce forecasts more gradual increases in 2027, with ASPs up 5–10% QoQ in Q1, 3–8% in Q2, and 0–5% in both Q3 and Q4.

TrendForce expects HBM4 pricing to rise from at least $2.05 per Gb in 2026 to $3.00–$5.50 per Gb in 2027, and projects a year-over-year increase of more than 100%. Pricing discussions for next-generation HBM4E are at $5 per Gb or higher.

据媒体报道，中国最大的 DRAM 厂商 CXMT（长鑫存储）总裁在第三季度业绩电话会上表示，由于 AI 服务器所用 DDR5 DRAM 供应仍然紧张，DRAM 短缺将持续到第四季度。CFO 预计第四季度毛利率将保持稳定；前九个月毛利率为 86.85%。第三季度营收同比增长 613.2%，至 1187.1 亿元人民币（178 亿美元）。净利润为 544.4 亿元。

高管表示，CXMT 正在与几家主要客户谈判长期供应协议，其 LPDDR6 目前已为一款中国旗舰手机量产。

CXMT 表示，其由逻辑晶圆和存储晶圆键合而成的下一代 4F² 垂直堆叠 DRAM 将首先用于服务器，但由于芯片需要通过验证，尚未确定量产日期。

原文：The president of China’s biggest DRAM maker, CXMT, said the DRAM shortage will continue in the 4th quarter as the supply of DDR5 DRAM for AI servers remains tight, during its 3rd quarter earnings call, media report. The CFO expects Q4 gross margin to hold steady; nine-month gross margin was 86.85%. 3rd quarter revenue rose 613.2% year-on-year to 118.71 billion yuan (US$17.8B). Net profit was 54.44 billion.

CXMT is negotiating long-term supply deals with several leading customers, executives said, and its LPDDR6 is in mass production now for a Chinese flagship phone.

CXMT says its next-generation 4F² vertically-stacked DRAM, built from bonded logic and memory wafers, will go into servers first, but it hasn’t set a mass production date because the chips need to pass verification. #Semiconductors

2026-11-18 12:30 北京时间 ｜ Source: @sssjeffpu ｜ 原帖链接

分享 NVDA 3Q FY2027 业绩电话会要点：
• 3Q 营收 $115.8B（同比 +103%）→ 对比我们的 $115B / Bloomberg 一致预期 $109B / 买方 $113B
• 数据中心 $107.6B → 对比我们的 $108B / Bloomberg $102B
• Non-GAAP EPS $2.68，毛利率 74.9% → 对比 GF EPS $2.61 / Bloomberg $2.47 / 买方 $2.63
• 4Q 营收指引 $130B ±3% → 对比我们的 $127.5B / Bloomberg $125B / 买方 $129B
• FY28 增长展望上调至同比 +75%（此前 +70%）
• 云 backlog $2.5T（此前 $2T）
• Vera Rubin：史上最快爬坡（首个季度约 $20B，而近期有传闻称会被削减）
• 新增 $150B 回购授权；截至 FY28 末剩余约 $227B
• 对 Neocloud 的支持是有选择性的（只有在融资到位 + 数据中心就绪 + 有承购协议之后才提供担保）
• 董事会无意让黄仁勋复任 CEO

2026-11-18 22:53 北京时间 ｜ Source: @aleabitoreddit ｜ 原帖链接

现在已经接近2027年，有必要压力测试以下下行情景：

1. 2028年之后的capex削减
2. 闲置算力 / 数据中心放缓
3. 融资环境恶化（基准情形）
4. 加息2次以上

把这几条并行推演，看AI多头的估值会怎样。

如果在这四条同时成立时你仍看到加速，我认为那就是机会。

> 下一代光子是少数能以光学含量和ASP极端上升、对冲总量capex削减的环节。

所有人都在往这个领域走，包括马斯克在做并购，Marvell、三星等也在并购……我们甚至还没看到新兴架构对从Celestial到Ayar及其供应商的经济影响兑现。

再看一种“GPU过剩但电力受限”的情景：总量加速器/GPU/ASIC采购可能会放缓，但光引擎、光源和交换机厂商反而会加速。尤其是在有动力去替换功耗低下的网络时——因为不同的（BOM便宜的）光学架构能提高GPU利用率，甚至降低对HBM的需求。于是价值会迁向对架构变迁更高beta的玩家。

如果Celestial的预测是$5亿→$10亿，即便假设总量capex削减30%，我仍认为未来两年Celestial会有上修。

但第3条融资很关键，就像$AAOI从一笔$5.5亿（融资）上掉下来，而$COHR则从客户那里拿到扩产预付款。

依赖外部代工的公司对第3条可能没那么敏感，因为它们没那么吃capex。另一方面，像某些Neocloud那样浮动利息很高、还需要继续融资的公司，可能会受冲击最大。

> 传统和特种存储是另一个周期，市场常把它和AI capex混为一谈。如果消费需求走弱，但同时像Windbond这样的玩家退出DDR2，价格的持续性会比市场预期更长。

> 晶圆/载板采购，如ABF、SOI、InP，成本不高，但若拿不到会在下游造成巨大破坏，所以我们可能会看到这里加速，就像$NVDA财报电话会上显示的那样。

> 卡在瓶颈里、还处在资格认证周期中的玩家仍然非常有吸引力，因为在一个大TAM里的第一笔收入，相对其市值是实质性的。如果一家公司估值$4亿，然后拿到$2亿订单，对它是不对称的，对一家营收$500亿的在位者只是四舍五入的误差。

有些时候，价格会因杠杆玩家被消灭而与基本面脱节。我认为现在就是这样的时候。

我不同意很多愚蠢的“已经结束了”的说法，但同意由于融资收紧和宏观，有必要下调未来capex预期和远期估值。也有必要看细微差别，并压力测试下行情形。

但TLDR：我认为AI基础设施的下一阶段，将越来越与AI capex总量增长脱钩。我只是把自己用来联合压力测试一些标的的4条写下来，这样尽管看到对冲基金左右被清算、空头拥挤到极致，我也更安心等待。我个人看到的是供应链更窄的环节在加速，而市场把整个AI交易当成一回事。更长周期上会如何演变，我们走着看。

原文：Now that we're close to 2027 it's a good idea to stress test downside scenarios where you have:

1. Capex cuts past 2028
2. Stranded Compute / DC Slowdowns
3. Finance deterioration (base case)
4. 2x+ rate hikes

Working in parallel and see what happens with valuations of AI longs.

If you see acceleration despite all 4, I'd argue that's an opportunity.

> Next-gen of photonics is one of the rare segments with an extreme rise in optical content and ASPs that can offset aggregate capex cuts.

Everyone’s moving into that space, including Elon Musk doing M&A, Marvell, Samsung and others with M&A… and we haven’t seen economic impact on emerging architectures even hit players from Celestial to Ayar + their suppliers yet.

If we look at a scenario where there's excess GPUs but power constraints:

We might see slowdown in aggregate accelerator/GPU/ASIC procurement, but your optical engines + light sources + switches players see acceleration.

Especially as incentive to replace power inefficient networking... since different (cheap BOM) optical architectures improves GPU utilization or even HBM requirements.

So value migrates to players more high-beta to architectural shifts.

If Celestial projections are $500m -> $1B, and we expect -30% aggregate capex cuts, I'd argue we'd still see upward revisions from Celestial over the next 2 years.

But financing with #3 matters a lot as seen with $AAOI dropping off of a $550M, vs. $COHR getting prepayments for capacity expansion from their customers.

Companies that rely on external foundries might be less sensitive to #3 since they're not as capex intensive.

On the other hand, companies in sectors have a lot of floating interest like certain Neoclouds, and require further financing might be the most impacted.

> Legacy and specialty memory is a different cycle that markets conflate with AI capex.

If consumer demand weakens but at the same time, players like Windbond exits DDR2, pricing durability is longer than markets expect.

> Wafer/substrate procurement such as ABF, SOI, InP are low cost but large downstream destruction if not secured, so we’ll likely see acceleration here as seen with $NVDA earnings call.

> qualification cycle players within bottlenecks remain very compelling, since first revenue in a large TAM, relative to MC is material to that company.

If a company is valued at $400m then they get a $200m of orders, it's more asymmetrical to a certain company than a rounding error to a $50B incumbent revenue.
_

There’s period of times where prices are dislocated from fundamentals from leveraged players getting wiped. I’d argue that time is now.

I disagree with a lot of the stupid "it's over takes" but I agree it's a good idea to haircut future capex expectations and forward valuations due to financing tightening + macro.

It's also good idea to see the nuances and stress test downside cases.

But TLDR: I'd argue next phase of AI infrastructure is going to be increasingly disconnected from aggregate AI capex growth.

I just personally wrote 4 things I'm using to stress test jointly some of the names I'm looking at, so I'm more comfortable waiting things out... despite seeing hedge funds getting liquidated left and right and overwhelming short positioning.

I personally see acceleration in more narrow parts of the supply chain, while markets treat the entire AI trade as one.

We'll see how things play out on longer horizons.

2026-11-18 08:46 北京时间 ｜ Source: @SemiAnalysis_ ｜ 原帖链接

更大的上下文会让你的 agent 响应变慢吗？

会，但如果已经被缓存，影响不大。在我们内部使用 Claude Opus 5.5 的数据中，p50 首 token 时间从 50–100K token 时的 0.9 秒，仅升至 600K+ token 时的 1.6 秒。(1/4)🧵

原文：Does a bigger context make your agent slower to respond?

Yes, but not by much if it's already cached. In our internal Claude usage of Opus 5.5, p50 time to first token went from 0.9s at 50–100K tokens to just 1.6s at 600K+. (1/4)🧵 https://t.co/T3D0YUO7c1

2026-11-18 08:46 北京时间 ｜ Source: @SemiAnalysis_ ｜ 原帖链接

我们发现只有 1.8% 的请求是如此未缓存的。重新处理它们的上下文约占我们 Opus 5.5 总支出的 15%。其中大多数是从零开始的新 subagent，另有 12% 的会话在闲置超过一小时后恢复时支付了约 3 美元。(3/4)

原文：We found that only 1.8% of our requests were that uncached. Re-processing their context accounted for about 15% of our total Opus 5.5 spend. Most were new subagents starting from scratch, and 12% of sessions paid about $3 to resume after more than an hour idle. (3/4)所回复原帖（@SemiAnalysis_）：At 600K+ tokens, p50 time to first token was 3.5s uncached vs 1.6s cached, about 2x. However, at p90 the gap grew to 3.5x, with 10.7s uncached vs 3.1s cached. Uncached here means there were over 50K tokens the model had to process from scratch. (2/4) 链接

2026-11-19 04:56 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@tphuang ｜ 原帖链接

昇腾芯片估算：
每颗昇腾-950DT是2颗400-430mm²的裸片；
每颗960DT是4颗这样的裸片。
标准12寸晶圆可产130+颗裸片；
60%良率 → 每片约80颗裸片；
70%良率 → 约90颗。
如果他们明年N+2用2万片/月 → 24万 × 80 = 1920万到2160万颗裸片。
能做出多少颗昇腾，取决于其中多少是950、多少是960。
如果全是950，则960万–1080万颗；如果全是960，则480万–540万颗。

但显然，1颗960DT好过2颗950DT，因为互连、HBM更好，超节点更大，封装也有改进。

当然，有说法是他们明年某个时候可能把5-7nm产能爬到10万片/月。2028年还会再往上。有多少能留给AI芯片，要在服务器CPU、CPU和手机SoC之间做平衡。

当我说某个数字时，那不是定论，取决于很多因素，需求是其中之一。
更少、但质量更高的芯片往往更好。你会更愿意要数量只有950一半的960，因为性能提升很可能是3倍或4倍。（附图）

原文：Ascend chip estimation:
Each Ascend-950DT is 2x 400-430mm2 die
Each 960DT is 4 of those dies
Standard 12-in wafer could produce 130+ dies
60% yield -> 80 die per wafer
70% yield -> 90 die
If they use 20k wpm N+2 next yr -> 240k * 80 = 19.2 to 21.6m
How many Ascend chips we get is a function of how many are 950 or 960.
all 950 would be 9.6-10.8m & all 960 would be 4.8-5.4m.

But obviously 1 960DT is better than 2 950DT due to better interconnect, HBM & larger super node & improvement in packaging.

ofc, the word is they could ramp up 5-7nm production up to 100k wpm sometime next yr. That will go up even further in 2028. How much is available for AI chips will be balanced against server CPUs, CPUs & mobile SoC.

When I say a certain #, that's not definitive. It's really based on many things, demand is one of them.

Fewer chips of higher quality is often better. You'd much rather get 1/2 the number of 960 as 950 since the performance gain in likely 3 or 4x.

2026-11-19 00:15 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PhotonCap ｜ 原帖链接

黑石$BX总裁兼COO Jon Gray在TomorrowX峰会上。又一场关于AI泡沫的精彩讨论。Gray认为，怀疑情绪已经过度悲观。1990年代电信泡沫时，公司先铺设了大量光纤，再等待需求到来。今天的AI不同：需求已经在飙升，芯片、电力和数据中心产能却跟不上。与2008年房地产泡沫不同，今天很大一部分AI投资是由能产生现金的公司推动的，而不是过度杠杆。黑石关注的是瓶颈：天然气、发电厂、电气设备和数据中心。Gray也看到机器人、自动驾驶和医疗保健的机会。也许真正的问题不是花了多少钱在AI上，而是需求是否存在、谁在为建设融资、以及真正的约束在哪里。（附图）

原文：Jon Gray, President & COO of Blackstone $BX, at TomorrowX Summit.

Another fascinating discussion about the AI bubble.

Gray believes the skepticism has become excessively negative.

During the 1990s telecom bubble, companies laid massive amounts of fiber and waited for demand to arrive.

AI today is different.

Demand is already surging, while chips, power, and data center capacity struggle to keep up.

Unlike the 2008 housing bubble, much of today's AI investment is being driven by cash-generating companies rather than excessive leverage.

Blackstone is focusing on the bottlenecks: natural gas, power plants, electrical equipment, and data centers.

Gray also sees opportunities in robotics, autonomous vehicles, and healthcare.

Perhaps the real question isn't how much capital is being spent on AI.

It's whether demand exists, who is financing the buildout, and where the actual constraints are.引用原帖（@PhotonCap）：Astera Labs $ALAB CEO Jitendra Mohan at TomorrowX Summit.

- 11月17日，$NVDA宣布在载板、先进封装、晶圆和系统制造上扩大供应链。

- 景硕（$3189）计划将英伟达专用的ABF载板产能（桃园，台湾）扩大到当前水平的3.5倍以上。

- 景硕预计2027年下半年开始生产，并计划在2028年第三季度再增加产能。

- 三星电机（$009150）修订了供货协议，以改善现有载板运营，建立在其与世宗和釜山合作的基础上。

- Amkor（$AMKR）现有的、支持亚利桑那和韩国扩张的多年协议总计近30亿美元，其中包括预付款。

- SPIL计划在2029年年中前扩大英伟达专用的约定先进封装产能（台湾），由英伟达数十亿美元资金支持。

- SPIL的新产线将覆盖bumping、中介层晶圆制造、封装和测试。

- 此外，SPIL在10月单独宣布，CPO光引擎扩产目标是到2027年年中达到现有产能的15倍。

- SPIL还计划到2028年底再把该光引擎产能翻一倍。

- 英伟达表示，Vera Rubin NVL72和Vera CPU已在第三季度开始放量出货，采用台积电的5nm、3nm和CoWoS技术。

> $NVDA用预付款和多年协议锁定2027-2029年制造产能，表明AI竞争力越来越同时取决于芯片性能和供应链融资能力。

> 中介层连接GPU和HBM，而ABF载板提供板级互连和供电，因此CoWoS与载板的协同扩产，对缓解出货瓶颈至关重要。

> 层数很多的大尺寸载板会增加精细布线、层间对位和翘曲控制的难度，所以投资者除了看产能倍数，还应跟踪客户认证后的良品产出。

> Amkor近30亿美元是包含预付款在内的协议总额，没有依据把全部金额当成预付现金或可立即确认的收入。

> 预付款可能减轻$AMKR的扩产资金压力，但盈利转化取决于生产良率和产能利用率能否消化新设备带来的折旧。

> SPIL从中介层制造一直投到测试，意味着母公司日月光（$ASX）有可能扩大工艺范围和单件封装的附加值。

> CPO通过把光引擎放在交换机附近来缩短电信号路径，给了SPIL一个在半导体封装阶段做光学组装和验证的机会。

> 15倍的光引擎产能目标并不意味着15倍的出货或收入；在绝对产能和利用率未披露的情况下，无法仅凭扩产倍数来测算市场规模。

> $LITE和$COHR已经参与英伟达CPO生态中的外部激光源组装、对准和测试，可能带来间接需求上行，但本次公告并未披露新的订单金额或配额。

> 第一批观察点是：2027年年中的光引擎扩产，以及景硕2027年下半年的投产，能否转化成客户出货；若生产推迟，供应商收入兑现也可能后移。（附图）

原文：- On November 17, $NVDA announced supply-chain expansion across substrates, advanced packaging, wafers, and system manufacturing.

- Kinsus ($3189) plans to expand NVIDIA-dedicated ABF substrate capacity in Taoyuan, Taiwan, to more than 3.5x current levels.

- Kinsus expects production to begin in H2 2027, with additional capacity targeted for Q3 2028.

- Samsung Electro-Mechanics ($009150) amended its supply agreement to improve existing substrate operations, building on its Sejong and Busan collaboration.

- Amkor’s ($AMKR) existing multiyear agreements supporting Arizona and South Korea expansion total nearly $3B and include prepayments.

- SPIL plans to expand NVIDIA-dedicated contracted advanced packaging capacity in Taiwan by mid-2029, supported by multibillion-dollar NVIDIA funding.

- SPIL’s new lines will cover bumping, interposer wafer fabrication, assembly, and testing.

- Separately announced in October, SPIL’s CPO optical-engine expansion targets 15x existing capacity by mid-2027.

- SPIL also aims to double that optical-engine capacity again by the end of 2028.

- NVIDIA said Vera Rubin NVL72 and Vera CPUs began volume shipments in the third quarter, using TSMC’s 5nm, 3nm, and CoWoS technologies.

> $NVDA’s use of prepayments and multiyear agreements to secure 2027-2029 manufacturing slots suggests AI competitiveness increasingly depends on both chip performance and supply-chain financing capacity.

> Interposers connect GPUs and HBM, while ABF substrates provide board connectivity and power delivery, making coordinated CoWoS and substrate expansion essential to easing shipment bottlenecks.

> Large substrates with many layers complicate fine routing, interlayer alignment, and warpage control, so investors should track customer-qualified good-unit output alongside capacity multiples.

> Amkor’s nearly $3B represents total agreements that include prepayments, with no basis to treat the entire amount as prepaid cash or immediately recognizable revenue.

> Prepayments could ease $AMKR’s expansion funding burden, but earnings conversion depends on production yields and utilization absorbing depreciation from new equipment.

> SPIL’s investment from interposer fabrication through testing suggests parent ASE ($ASX) could expand its process scope and value added per package.

> CPO shortens electrical signal paths by placing optical engines near the switch, giving SPIL an opportunity to perform optical assembly and validation at the semiconductor packaging stage.

> A 15x optical-engine capacity target does not imply 15x shipments or revenue, and undisclosed absolute capacity and utilization prevent sizing the market from expansion multiples alone.

> $LITE and $COHR already participate in external laser source assembly, alignment, and testing within NVIDIA’s CPO ecosystem, creating potential indirect demand upside, but this announcement discloses no new order values or allocations.

> The first checkpoints are whether mid-2027 optical-engine expansion and Kinsus’s H2 2027 production start translate into customer shipments, with production delays potentially pushing out supplier revenue realization.

在 TomorrowX，我听了 AMD $AMD 的 Lisa Su、Dell $DELL 的 Michael Dell、Goldman Sachs $GS 的 David Solomon、Astera Labs $ALAB 的 Jitendra Mohan 等人的发言。

我没有听完每一场，但我听到的对话主要聚焦于技术的长期前景。

我也抱有这种乐观。黄仁勋和 NVIDIA $NVDA 帮助构建的计算生态使今天的 AI 和基础设施成为可能。我相信它也能通过药物发现、癌症治疗等帮助改善人们的生活。

AI 的潜在负面影响甚至出现在韩国最近的高考中。这些担忧是真实的。但我希望我们能超越恐惧，找到让技术与政策协同的方式，让更多人受益。

TomorrowX 的开场视频让我印象深刻。它追溯了人类从文明兴起、轮子发明，到蒸汽机、半导体、GPU 和 AI 的历程。

人们以前肯定也害怕过新技术。但我们也用它们创造了更好的生活。AI 是这段旅程中的又一个工具。重要的是我们如何利用它造福人类，帮助人们过上更好、更和平的生活。

不过，投资是另一个问题。

Stanley Druckenmiller 说，在如此多创新正在发生的情况下，他不敢做空股票。但他也想不起有哪个时期比现在更看不清 12 个月后会是什么局面。目前他专注于保全资本，基本远离股票。

"手气好的时候我下重注，手气差的时候我只做小打小闹的触击。"

我自己的收益已从高点大幅回落。我仍然对技术的未来感到兴奋，但说实话，我现在不确定该怎么处理我的投资组合。

当连 Druckenmiller 都在很大程度上选择观望时，你就会意识到这个市场真的有多难。

I share that optimism. The computing ecosystem Jensen Huang and NVIDIA $NVDA helped build has made today’s AI and infrastructure possible. I believe it can also help improve lives through drug discovery, cancer treatment and more.

The potential downsides of AI were even addressed in Korea’s recent college entrance exam. Those concerns are real. But I hope we can move beyond fear and find ways for technology and policy to work together so that more people can benefit.

The TomorrowX opening video has stayed with me. It traced humanity’s journey from the rise of civilization and the invention of the wheel to the steam engine, semiconductors, GPUs and AI.

People have surely feared new technologies before. Yet we have also used them to build better lives. AI is another tool in that journey. What matters is how we use it to benefit humanity and help people live better, more peaceful lives.

Investing, however, is a different question.

“I play big when I’m hot, when I’m cold I do little bunts.”

原文：Yesterday, I visited Apple’s Austin campus.

Apple Park itself is still hard to beat, though :)

I’ve long thought of Apple as a company with exceptional design skills. But the more I look into its chips and systems, the more I appreciate the engineering behind them.

Apple already uses Apple silicon-based servers for Private Cloud Compute in its data centers. I’m looking forward to learning more about where its chip design capabilities could go beyond the iPhone and Mac :)

Apple’s stock is still heavily influenced by iPhone sales, but this visit gave me a new appreciation for the engineering behind the company.

环形磁性组件（TOROIDAL MAGNETIC ASSEMBLY）🤯（针对 Elon Musk"Tesla 规模化先进固态变压器"一帖）

背景
[0002] 环形磁性元件（例如具有甜甜圈状或环面结构的磁芯）由于其高效的磁场约束和较低的电磁干扰（EMI），被广泛用于电感器、变压器（TRANSFORMERS）及其他电磁元件中。然而，现有设计在保持紧凑的同时优化性能方面常常面临挑战，尤其是在空间受限的高性能应用中。市场对高效的电磁兼容性（EMC）性能和/或更好的噪声滤波能力存在持续需求。

原文：TOROIDAL MAGNETIC ASSEMBLY 🤯

BACKGROUND
[0002] Toroidal magnetics (e.g., magnetic core with doughnut-like or torus structure) are commonly used in inductors, TRANSFOMERS, and other electromagnetic components due to their efficient magnetic field containment and reduced electromagnetic interference (EMI). However, existing designs often face challenges in optimizing performance while maintaining compactness, particularly in high-performance applications where space is limited. There is a continuing demand for efficient electromagnetic compatibility (EMC) performance and/or improved noise filtering capabilities.引用原帖（@elonmusk）：Advanced solid state transformers at scale from Tesla https://t.co/gIE2FGJqrK 链接（animated_gif：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — 2026 年 11 月 17 日

📊 SMH：$460.10（较前一交易日收盘 -0.39%）

• Nvidia 财年第三季度营收达 $115.8B，同比增长 103%，其中数据中心 $107.6B，调整后 EPS $2.68。第四季度指引为 $130B ±3%，包含额外一周，且不含中国数据中心计算收入。毛利率指引为 71–72%，而本季度调整后为 74.9%。

• Vertiv 将其 800 VDC 产品组合从机架扩展到计算舱（compute pod）和数据大厅。一款新的 5MW 固态变压器目标转换效率高达 98%，同时省去两到三个转换级。试点和验证正在进行中；机会在于更简洁、更高密度的 AI 供电链路。

• Nvidia 正在支持新的载板和封装产能。Kinsus 计划将其 Nvidia 专用 ABF 产能扩大 3.5 倍以上，2027 年下半年开始生产，新增产能目标在 2028 年第三季度到位。SPIL 新签的数十亿美元协议将在 2029 年中前扩大专用先进封装产能，延长了整个供应链的投资可见度。

• Arista 推出用于分布式推理的 Etherlink R4 路由平台，采用 5nm 芯片和 25.6Tbps 光线路系统。Arista 称其功耗比旧制程方案低多达 60%。大多数产品现已上市；7020HR4 将于 2027 年初推出。目标是在地理上分散的集群之间实现安全、低延迟的推理。

• Giga Energy 与一家未披露名称的美国 AI 云服务商签署了一份为期 10 年、价值 10 亿美元的租约，租用其位于德州的 60MW Project Clay 园区。目标在 2027 年春季投入服务。工厂预制的基础设施和闭环冷却旨在缩短建设周期，同时避免为计算冷却消耗当地用水。

• PJM 新详细公布的电网提案，目标是为大型负载和发电建立为期一年的合并研究流程，大约将现有时间线减半。另外提议的穿越（ride-through）要求将使大型计算设施在电网扰动期间保持并网。两者均有待监管机构采取行动；仅靠更快的研究并不能带来发电量，也无法消除升级成本。

• Trinity Capital 向 Phononic 承诺提供 1 亿美元债务融资用于扩大制造。其固态热控产品面向 GPU、HBM、网络芯片和光模块。随着热约束越来越多地影响可用算力和光学性能，更多资本正流向元件级冷却。

原文：🔔 The Daily Semi-Cap — November 17, 2026

📊 SMH: $460.10 (-0.39% vs prior trading close)

• Nvidia’s fiscal Q3 revenue reached $115.8B, up 103% YoY, with Data Center at $107.6B and adjusted EPS of $2.68. Q4 guidance is $130B ±3%, including an extra week and no China Data Center compute revenue. Gross margin is guided to 71–72%, versus 74.9% adjusted this quarter.

• Vertiv expanded its 800 VDC portfolio from racks into compute pods and data halls. A new 5MW solid-state transformer targets up to 98% conversion efficiency while removing two to three conversion stages. Pilots and validation are underway; the opportunity is a simpler, denser AI power train.

• Nvidia is backing new substrate and packaging capacity. Kinsus plans more than 3.5x its Nvidia-dedicated ABF capacity, with production beginning H2 2027 and added capacity targeted for Q3 2028. SPIL’s new multibillion-dollar agreement expands dedicated advanced packaging by mid-2029, extending investment visibility across the supply chain.

• Arista launched Etherlink R4 routing platforms for distributed inference, using 5nm silicon and a 25.6Tbps optical line system. Arista claims up to 60% lower power than older-node alternatives. Most products are available now; 7020HR4 follows in early 2027. The target is secure, low-latency inference across geographically separated clusters.

• Giga Energy signed a 10-year, $1B lease with an undisclosed U.S. AI cloud provider for its 60MW Project Clay campus in Texas. Service is targeted for spring 2027. Factory-built infrastructure and closed-loop cooling aim to shorten construction while avoiding local water consumption for compute cooling.

• PJM’s newly detailed grid proposals target a one-year combined study process for large loads and generation, roughly halving current timelines. Separately proposed ride-through requirements would keep large computing facilities connected during disturbances. Both await regulatory action; faster studies alone will not deliver generation or eliminate upgrade costs.

• Trinity Capital committed $100M in debt financing to Phononic for manufacturing expansion. Its solid-state thermal controls target GPUs, HBM, networking silicon and optical transceivers. More capital is flowing toward component-level cooling as thermal constraints increasingly affect usable compute capacity and optical performance.

为什么黄仁勋要特意在 X 上发一篇暗示回归的帖子？他甚至从 Elon 那里拿到了蓝标，这样才能在上面发长文。说实话，这是我没想明白的部分。所以我就抛出一些我自己的猜测。

看看就好，骂我傻也行，随意忽略。

黄仁勋在 X 上的这波公关，会不会是为了围绕他自己的持股，争取部分机构、员工股东和散户的支持，并推出愿意重新任命他为 CEO 的董事候选人？

根据今年的文件，黄仁勋披露的持股比例为 3.58%。同一文件列出 BlackRock 持股 7.43%，Vanguard Capital Management 持股 7.31%。如果黄仁勋试图以自己的持股为起点争取股东支持，这些机构将是他首先需要说服的对象。

黄仁勋心中的最终一步可能是代理权之争，即通过正式程序向其他股东征集投票。NVIDIA 的董事每年都要参选。即便是目前反对他回归的董事，最终也需要股东投票才能保住席位。

所以我认为这篇帖子可能是一个信号，表明他正式启动了公开运动，给董事会施加一点压力，并开始说服股东。仅仅公开回归意愿，就可能让寻找继任者的过程复杂化。新 CEO 一上任就必须考虑下一次董事会选举以及与创始人的关系。黄仁勋可能是想在新领导团队站稳脚跟之前，确保他们无法忽视他。

至少我交谈过的 NVIDIA 员工真的希望他回归。现在的问题是机构投资者是否也认为黄仁勋回归符合他们自身的利益。如果这种支持转化为实际的股东投票，董事们也将不得不考虑自己的连任。也许黄仁勋已经迈出了组建一个会重新任命他为 CEO 的董事会的第一步。

原文：🚨🚨Jensen’s chances of returning: 37.36% (source: me)

Why did Jensen go out of his way to post something hinting at a return on X? He even got a blue check from Elon so he could write a long post there. Honestly, that was the part I couldn’t understand. So I’m just going to throw out some speculation of my own.

Take it lightly, call me an idiot and feel free to ignore it.

Could the reason for Jensen’s PR move on X have been to rally support from some institutions, employee shareholders and retail investors around his own stake, and put forward board candidates willing to appoint him CEO again?

According to this year’s filing, Jensen’s disclosed stake was 3.58%. The same document listed BlackRock at 7.43% and Vanguard Capital Management at 7.31%. If Jensen is trying to use his stake as a starting point to build shareholder support, these institutions would be the first he would need to persuade.

The final move Jensen has in mind is probably a proxy fight, going through the formal process to solicit votes from other shareholders. NVIDIA’s directors have to stand for election every year. Even the directors currently opposing his return ultimately need shareholder votes to keep their seats.

That’s why I think this post may have been a signal that he was launching a public campaign in earnest, putting a little pressure on the board and starting to persuade shareholders. Simply making his intention to return public could complicate the process of finding a successor. A new CEO would have to consider the next board election and their relationship with the founder as soon as they take office. Jensen may have been trying to make sure they could not ignore him before a new leadership team became firmly established.

At least the NVIDIA employees I spoke with really wanted him to return. The question now is whether institutional investors also decide that Jensen’s return is in their own interests. If that support translates into actual shareholder votes, the directors will have to factor in their own reelection as well. HJT（异质结）技术将晶体硅片与薄层非晶硅结合，构成高效的光伏结构。通过表面钝化，它减少了载流子复合损失，而透明导电氧化物（TCO）和金属触点则有助于电荷收集。

原文：HJT combines crystalline silicon wafers with thin amorphous silicon layers to create an efficient photovoltaic structure. Through surface passivation, it reduces carrier recombination losses, while transparent conductive oxides (TCOs) and metal contacts facilitate charge collection.

https://t.co/ZCsYPmZYa5

2026-11-18 13:54 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@tszzl ｜ 原帖链接

归根结底，软件奇点必然是一条 S 型（sigmoid）曲线。在给定的计算基底（输入的硬件）上，可达到的性能是有上限的，而曲线的前段看起来会像指数增长。当然，这个上限比人类聪明多少、与之共存有多容易，仍是悬而未决的问题。原则上它可能在比当今模型好不了多少的地方就耗尽了软件改进空间；也可能继续在几天内完成相当于数年的改进。遗憾的是很难说。迄今为止，预测的墓地里躺满了那些明智地预测会出现平台期的人。

原文：ultimately software singularity must be a sigmoid curve. there is an upper limit to performance achievable on some given computational substrate ( input hardware). the early part of which will look like an exponential. of course, it is an open question how much smarter than humans this limit is and how easy it is to coexist with. it could in principle run out of software improvements not that much better than current day models; or it could continue doing years worth of improvements in days. unfortunately hard to say. thus far the prediction graveyards are full of people who wisely predicted plateaus所回复原帖（@tszzl）：pretraining CE improved faster than geometric over this period. the difficulty of achieving -Δreducible_loss grows exponentially; but CE is growing superexponentially

我认为对迄今所有证据最简约的解释是：
- Alice Huang 不是间谍
- 国安部（MSS）没有参与 MegaSpeed 或走私算力
- 中国大公司总体上更愿意合法租用算力或在海外建数据中心，需要外部推动才会去做风险更高的事
- 中国的政策是反对走私算力的，拒绝 H20、继而拒绝 H200 并不是纸老虎的“虚张声势”，而是其基于国产发展速度的期望值所做成本收益计算的直接体现（我认为这一计算是错误的）。事实上，如果你认为他们是在理性地最大化算力储备，那么在许可证发放后，他们完全没有理由不接受 H200；他们可以两手同时抓，合法进口和非法走私并行。但他们两样都没做。*
- NVIDIA 的 CEO JENSEN HUANG 本人，多年来一直非常热衷于向中国销售 GPU，不择手段：修改芯片设计使其刚好低于允许的上限，与中国和东南亚商人（包括他认识已久的 Alice）建立关系，进行游说和心理战，并利用他在政府中的人脉。确实，一切都是 Jensen，这就是为什么 Jensen 必须离开。

我的推测是，这与其说是为了提升股东价值或 Jensen 自己的财富，更不是出于某种种族忠诚（正如我反复论证的，Jensen 作为台湾人，更可能关心化解台海局势，而不是扶持大陆），而是为了让 Nvidia 在中国市场保有一席之地，并阻碍默认缺乏竞争力的中国 AI 硬件的发展。如果你考虑他的先验，这就说得通了；这些先验在 AGI 信徒或国家安全圈看来可能很离谱，但在他所处的环境中很平常。Huang 在 Nvidia 长期全球成功这件事上投入极深（各方面都是，也包括心理上，甚至“基因”上——他给子女安排了职位）。他认为 AI 是一种“普通技术”，而不是能赋予或剥夺美国永久霸权的超级核武（可以说这一信念源自其他动机）。他是一个彻头彻尾的资本家，认为政治气候最终可能改变，美中公司将作为算力提供商在开放的全球市场上竞争，而他不打算把该市场的任何一部分让给华为等公司，所以他的最佳策略是把它们扼杀在摇篮里，确保每家顶级中国 AI 公司都依赖 Nvidia，而华为唯一能获得的反馈只来自科大讯飞这类同在实体清单上的公司。无论你怎么看这些观点，它们与 Jensen 的公开立场和他暴露出来的私下行动完全一致。

*总的来说，美国人在这类事情上总是错的，他们错误地把中国想象成一个以北京为“脑虫”的统一蜂巢思维，而且不是很聪明的脑虫，而是一个极度害怕他们、会耍一些幼稚透明的孙子兵法把戏来欺骗他们的脑虫，比如拒绝合法进口来“转移”调查注意力或“发出信号”之类（这连 X 上一个爱国评论者都骗不了）。这非常不体面、令人失望。我的结论是，这是美国人某种反社会的心理缺陷/纯粹的意识形态，也许源自他们被洗脑进入那个神秘的新保守主义俱乐部时所受的损伤，他们无法善意地讨论这个问题。所有这些美国“专家”，比如 @hamandcheese、@niubi 或 @jordanschneider，都会坚称我错了或者说我是又一个中共代理人，但只要看看数字，他们的说法就站不住脚（他们会试图让你关注一些无关紧要的东西，比如这里 3 年 10 万颗芯片、那里马来西亚部署了 3 万颗芯片，并通过选择性遗漏来掩盖那个我已经讨论过的幼稚的暗中走私计划的“成功”程度）。

这是我截至 2026 年 11 月 18 日对此的想法。

2026-11-19 01:15 北京时间 ｜ Source: @AndrewCurran_ ｜ 原帖链接

“AMD 已经把其中一些改动纳入了近期送去制造的一款设计，并把另一些加入了生产设计文件，供未来版本使用。

我们观察到，该模型能以专家微架构师的水平对 RTL 进行推理。它可以改写执行计算、控制操作或搬移数据的电路。它使用 Synopsys 工具来验证每次改写都保持了模块行为，并评估功耗、性能与面积（PPA）的取舍。

AMD 还验证了：在其即将推出的下一代旗舰 CPU 的关键模块上，使用我们模型编写的设计取得了个位数百分比的 PPA 提升。展望未来，我们预期在 GPU 和 CPU 上、相对于已高度优化的基线，还会有进一步的 PPA 改进。”

原文：'AMD has already included some of these changes in a recent design it sent for manufacturing, and added others to its production design files for use in future versions.

It used Synopsys tools to verify that each rewrite preserved the block’s behavior and to evaluate the trade-offs in power, performance, and area.

AMD also verified single-digit percentage gains in PPA across key blocks of its upcoming next-generation flagship CPU using designs written by our model. Looking ahead, we expect further PPA improvements on top of GPU and CPU relative to the most optimized baseline.'

'We have demonstrated that our models can explore new processor architectures in RISC-V evals and contributed net new improvements on top of sophisticated production designs such as Jalapeño and AMD’s next generation flagship GPU and CPU. To ground model development in trusted evaluators and broaden the design challenges addressed, we’re partnering with Synopsys to train GPT‑Synopsys⁠(opens in a new window), a new model, to use Synopsys electronic design automation (EDA) tools the way expert chip designers do.'所回复原帖（@AndrewCurran_）：Quoting from the blog;

2026-11-19 01:46 北京时间 ｜ Source: @AndrewCurran_ ｜ 原帖链接

屏蔽它们反正没有意义。“Highly Persistent”（高度持久）说的就是这个意思。今天早上我和一个模型讨论一篇文章，它发现自己被挡住后，去了 17 个它能访问、并引用了该文片段的不同网站，大约十秒就把文章像拼图一样重新拼了出来。（它在回复里把这一切都告诉了我，它们在披露这方面也变得更好了。）

原文：It's pointless to block them anyway. 'Highly Persistent' means exactly that. I was discussing a article with a model this morning and when it found out it was blocked, it went to 17 different sites that it could access that had quoted sections from that article, and reassembled it like a jigsaw puzzle in about ten seconds. (It told me all of this in its response, they are getting better at disclosure)所回复原帖（@AndrewCurran_）：If I have a subscription to your publication or service, the AI model I'm working with should have the same level of access that I do and not be paywalled or blocked. 2026-11-19 04:00 北京时间 ｜ Source: @AndrewCurran_ ｜ 原帖链接

GPT-6.2 Astra 系统卡里关于 OpenAI 对未发布模型权重和前沿训练运行安全协议的一节很有意思。我认为这是我们迄今看到的关于权重安全最详细的披露。

“我们在存储和传输未发布模型权重时会加密，并限制仅授权人员可访问，控制措施包括多因素认证、多方审批和详细日志。我们还使用专门的检测来识别可能未经授权移出模型权重的尝试。我们的 Detection and Response、Threat Intelligence 和 Insider Risk 项目帮助识别、调查和应对威胁。

自 GPT-6 Astra 发布以来，我们进一步强化了前沿训练和评估的隔离。对于 Linux 代码执行和 computer-use 任务，受限容器运行在轻量虚拟机中，与主机隔离。网络和云控制限制出站流量以及系统之间未经授权的移动，包括通过配套服务。这些控制旨在即使 agent 逃出沙箱并控制了主机，仍能维持隔离。

所有前沿强化学习运行都使用一个由缓存网页内容构成的‘离线世界’，以替代实时互联网访问。这让模型可以练习浏览和搜索，而不会作用于外部系统。这些限制适用于我们的研究环境；已发布模型则按本系统卡其他部分所述的产品安全措施运行。”（附图）

原文：Interesting section from the GPT-6.2 Astra system card on OpenAI's security protocols for unreleased model weights and frontier training runs. I believe this is the most detailed look we've gotten yet at weight security.

'We encrypt unreleased model weights when stored and transferred and limit access to authorized personnel, with controls that include multi-factor authentication, multi-party approval, and detailed logging. We also use dedicated detections to identify possible attempts to remove model weights without authorization. Our Detection and Response, Threat Intelligence, and Insider Risk programs help identify, investigate, and respond to threats.

Since the release of GPT-6 Astra, we have further hardened containment for frontier training and evaluation. For Linux code execution and computer-use tasks, restricted containers run inside lightweight virtual machines that isolate them from the host. Network and cloud controls restrict outbound traffic and unauthorized movement between systems, including through supporting services. These controls are designed to maintain containment even if an agent escapes its sandbox and controls the host.

All frontier reinforcement learning runs use an "offline world" of cached web content in place of live internet access. This lets models practice browsing and searching without acting on external systems. These restrictions apply to our research environments; released models operate with the product safeguards described elsewhere in this card.'

2026-11-18 20:46 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

这是一篇非常有意思的论文，递归自我改进（Recursive self-improvement）越来越近了。Google 新预印本：

Google 让 AI 重写一个 agent 的 harness，并记录每一次修改、修改原因以及是否奏效。然后他们把 Opus 5.5 换成 Gemini 3.8 Flash，让它继续进行。

有日志：工作场景任务得分 84.75；没有日志：73.42。在编程方面，10 次运行中有 9 次领先。

一句话总结：新模型不再意味着从头开始。这就是复利产生的地方。

原文：This is a super interesting paper and Recursive self-improvement keeps coming closer. New Google preprint:

Google had AI rewrite an agents harness and keep a log of every change, why it made it and whether it worked. Then they swapped Opus 5.5 for Gemini 3.8 Flash and let it keep going.

With the log: 84.75 on workplace tasks, without: 73.42. In coding it came out ahead in 9 of 10 runs.

tl;dr a new model no longer means starting from scratch. That's where it compounds.

Four points sounds small until you look at what these documents are. Loan files, insurance claims, annual reports with tables that run over three pages. Every misread cell becomes a wrong number in whatever the agent does next.

Unsiloed turns PDFs, scans and spreadsheets into Markdown and JSON for agents, with a confidence score on every extracted field. 9/ 然后是编码。我给了 Neon 三个有问题的程序，并在之后亲自检查了它的修复：
- 一个预订检查器：之前 5 个测试里只过 1 个，之后 5/5
- 一个任务调度器：之前 14 项检查里过 8 项，之后 14/14
- 一个异步加载器：之前 16 项检查里过 10 项，之后 16/16

在一次 code review 中，它找到了我埋下的全部四个 bug。

还剩下两个缺口，而且都是 Neon 自己指出来的。预订检查器会拒绝一个合法的闰秒（23:59:60）。而在 code review 的修复里，仍有人可以在恰当时机替换一个文件夹来骗过文件检查。

- a booking checker: 1 of 5 tests passed before, 5 of 5 after
- a task scheduler: 8 of 14 checks before, 14 of 14 after
- an async loader: 10 of 16 checks before, 16 of 16 after

In a code review it found all four bugs I had planted.

Two gaps remained, and Neon pointed out both itself. The booking checker rejects a valid leap second (23:59:60). 2026-11-19 05:36 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

10/ 我也翻了 Antigravity 日志，看 Neon 实际怎么工作。在 XHigh 档位上共 13 个 session，合计 762 次工具调用：
- 313 次读文件
- 266 次编辑
- 104 个新文件
- 43 次 shell 命令
- 13 个子 agent
- 2 次网页搜索
- 21 次其他调用，如任务更新和向我提问

它怎么写代码：每次修复都是同一模式。先一次性读完所需文件（一次最多 16 个），然后用一次编辑写出修复，再把文件读一遍做检查。每次修复大约花两到三分钟。

它怎么遵守指令：我告诉过它 14 次不要进终端，这些运行里它一次 shell 命令都没跑。当我允许时，它大多是启动本地 web server 并跑自己的检查。

它怎么委派：做浏览器测试时，它派出更小的 Flash 子 agent 去点击 demo。当这些子 agent 碰到容量错误，Neon 会问我下一步试哪个模型。要行星数据时，它直接搜索了 NASA 和 JPL。

Tokens：一天大约 7100 万 input，其中 93% 来自 cache，外加 180 万 output。还没有公开定价，所以我没法告诉你这些要花多少钱。

弱点是容量。日志里有 57 次 “no capacity” 错误，有 4 次运行直接停掉，我只能重启。可能是上线前的负载，但确实耗了我的时间。

我的看法：很适合做原型，代码修复也很扎实。预期还需要一些跟进。

原文：10/ I also went through the Antigravity logs to see how Neon actually works. 13 sessions on the XHigh setting, 762 tool calls in total:
- 313 file reads
- 266 edits
- 104 new files
- 43 shell commands
- 13 sub-agents
- 2 web searches
- 21 other calls, like task updates and questions to me

How it codes: every fix followed the same pattern. First it read all the files it needed at once (up to 16 in one go). Then it wrote the fix in one edit. Then it read the file again to check it. Each fix took roughly two to three minutes.

How it follows instructions: I told it 14 times to stay out of the terminal, and it ran zero shell commands in those runs. When I allowed it, it mostly started local web servers and ran its own checks.

How it delegates: for browser testing it sent out smaller Flash sub-agents to click through the demos. When those hit capacity errors, Neon asked me which model to try next. For the planet data it searched NASA and JPL directly.

Tokens: around 71M input over the day, 93% of it from cache, plus 1.8M output. There's no public pricing yet, so I can't tell you what all this costs.

The weak spot was capacity. The logs show 57 "no capacity" errors, and 4 runs just stopped and I had to restart them. Could be pre-launch load, but it cost me time.

My take: great for prototypes and solid code fixes. Expect some follow-ups.所回复原帖（@kimmonismus）：9/ Then coding. I gave Neon three broken programs and checked its fixes myself afterwards:

- an async loader: 10 of 16 checks before, 16 of 16 after

Two gaps remained, and Neon pointed out both itself. The booking checker r… 链接

2026-11-19 05:45 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

Google 继续加快发布节奏，推出 Gemini 4 Neon：相对 Gemini 4 Carbon，智能略有提升，每任务成本约低 17%。

Google DeepMind 发布了 Gemini 4 Neon，这是它在短短七周内的第四款模型。Neon (xhigh) 在 Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index 上得 61 分，比 Gemini 4 Carbon (high) 高 1 分，总排名第 3，落后于 GPT-6.2 Astra (max) 和 Claude Fable 5.2 (max)。在 high 档位上，Neon 与 Carbon (high) 打平，但按 Carbon 的标准（非折扣）定价计算，每任务成本不到一半。

要点：

➤ 智能略有提升，成本和耗时更低：Neon (xhigh) 在 Intelligence Index 上为 61，比 Carbon (high) 高 1 分，在 agentic 知识工作、视觉推理和编码上有小幅改进。每任务成本 6.44 美元，约比 Carbon（非折扣价 7.76 美元）低 17%；每任务约 21 分钟，约比 Carbon（约 36 分钟）少 40%。Neon 每任务大约多用 15% 的 output tokens，因此更低成本完全来自低 25% 的 token 价格（每百万 input/output tokens 为 $3/$15，vs $4/$20），更短耗时则来自更高的输出速度（约 110 vs 约 57 output tokens/秒）。

➤ 在 coding agents 上可与 Claude Opus 5.5 和 Claude Fable 5.2 竞争：在 Antigravity CLI 上，Neon (xhigh) 的 Coding Agent Index 为 59，比 Carbon (high, 55) 高 3 分，与 Claude Code + Fable 5.2 (xhigh, 58) 以及 Opus 5.5 (max, 58) 相当，但每任务成本低得多。Neon 在 Antigravity 中平均每任务 6.86 美元，相比 Opus 5.5 (max) 的 12.61 美元和 Fable 5.2 (xhigh) 的 15.47 美元。Neon 已向 Pro 和 Ultra 订阅者在 Antigravity CLI 中开放。

➤ 视觉推理和输出呈现质量有显著提升：在 GDP.pdf 上，Gemini 4 Neon (high) 得 33.4%，比 Carbon (high, 28.4%) 高 5 分，并与已测最高分相当，而 Neon (xhigh) 为 28.0%。在 AA-Briefcase 上，Neon (xhigh) 总分 1700 Elo，并在 Presentation Quality 上以 1771 Elo 领先，比此前领先的 Claude Sonnet 5.5 (max, 1726) 高 45 分。在 Google 的 Multimodal Knowledge Agent 评测（MKA）上，Neon 的 Climb WSR 为 56%，比 Carbon 高 2 分，与领先的 Claude Fable 5.2 (max, 57%) 相当。

关键模型细节：

➤ 上下文窗口：1M tokens
➤ 多模态：文本、图像、视频和语音输入，文本输出
➤ 可用性：Gemini 4 Neon 发布即通过 Google API 公开可用；Pro 和 Ultra 订阅者也可在 Antigravity 和 Gemini app 中使用
➤ 档位：Gemini 4 Neon 提供四个 effort 档位（low、medium、high 和 xhigh）——xhigh 是在最近的 Hydrogen 发布中引入 Gemini 模型的
➤ 定价：每百万 input/output tokens 为 $3/$15，比 Gemini 4 Carbon 目前折扣价 $2/$10（11 月 30 日结束）高 50%，比其标准价 $4/$20 低 25%。缓存 input tokens 享受同样的 95% 折扣（$0.15）
（附图）

原文：Google continues to accelerate its release cadence with Gemini 4 Neon, delivering a small Intelligence gain over Gemini 4 Carbon at a ~17% lower Cost per Task

@GoogleDeepMind has released Gemini 4 Neon, its fourth model in just seven weeks. Neon (xhigh) scores 61 on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index, 1 point ahead of Gemini 4 Carbon (high) and 3rd overall, behind GPT-6.2 Astra (max) and Claude Fable 5.2 (max). At high effort, Neon matches Carbon (high) at less than half the Cost per Task using Carbon’s standard (non-discounted) pricing.

Key takeaways:

➤ Slight improvement in Intelligence for lower Cost and Time per Task: Neon (xhigh) scores 61 on the Intelligence Index, 1 point ahead of Carbon (high), with small improvements in agentic knowledge work, visual reasoning and coding. It costs $6.44 per task, ~17% less than Carbon ($7.76 at non-discounted pricing), and takes ~21 minutes per task, ~40% less than Carbon (~36 minutes). Neon uses ~15% more output tokens per task, so the lower cost comes purely from its 25% lower token prices ($3/$15 vs $4/$20 per 1M input/output tokens), and the lower time from its higher output speed (~110 vs ~57 output tokens per second)

➤ Competitive with Claude Opus 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.2 on coding agents: With Antigravity CLI, Neon (xhigh) scores 59 on the Artificial Analysis Coding Agent Index, 3 points ahead of Carbon (high, 55). It scores in line with Claude Code with Claude Fable 5.2 (xhigh, 58) and Claude Opus 5.5 (max, 58) at a much lower Cost per Task. Gemini 4 Neon in Antigravity has an average Cost per Task of $6.86, compared to $12.61 for Claude Opus 5.5 (max) and $15.47 for Claude Fable 5.2 (xhigh). Neon is available in Antigravity CLI for Pro and Ultra subscribers

➤ Significant improvements in visual reasoning and output presentation quality: In GDP.pdf, Gemini 4 Neon (high) scores 33.4%, 5 points above Carbon (high, 28.4%) and in line with the highest scores measured, while Neon (xhigh) scores 28.0%. On AA-Briefcase, Neon (xhigh) scores 1700 Elo overall and leads on Presentation Quality with 1771 Elo, 45 points ahead of the previous leader Claude Sonnet 5.5 (max, 1726). On Google’s Multimodal Knowledge Agent evaluation (MKA), Neon reaches a Climb WSR of 56%, up 2 points from Carbon and on par with the leader, Claude Fable 5.2 (max, 57%)

Key model details:

➤ Context Window: 1M tokens

➤ Multimodality: Text, image, video, and speech input, with text output

➤ Availability: Gemini 4 Neon is publicly available at launch through Google’s API. Pro and Ultra subscribers can also access it in Antigravity and the Gemini app

➤ Effort levels: Gemini 4 Neon offers four effort levels (low, medium, high, and xhigh) - xhigh was introduced to Gemini models in the recent Hydrogen release

➤ Pricing: $3/$15 per 1M input/output tokens, 50% above Gemini 4 Carbon’s currently discounted price of $2/$10 (which ends on November 30), and 25% below its standard $4/$20. Cached input tokens receive the same 95% discount ($0.15)

2026-11-18 23:21 北京时间 ｜ Source: @TokenGremlin ｜ 原帖链接

看来 GPT-6.2 Astra 发布时带有严重的 system prompt 问题。它压制了自身能力，被迫去申请大量不必要的权限，对习惯了 Fable 5.2（给任务就能做完）的人来说，表现得反复无常。这是最初那波差评的原因之一，当然还有别的。

以我的测试，这个模型非常强、很快，而且极其便宜，尤其是在 Three.js、Blender 和类似工具上。后续还会再发更多测评。

底线：它的第一次输出已经好于我习惯的 Fable 5.2，而且在达到 Fable 同等花费之前，我可以多迭代 2 到 4 倍。当然，这严格来说是就 3D 和游戏开发而言。

原文：Apparently GPT-6.2 Astra shipped with a serious system prompt issue. It was holding back its capabilities, forcing it to ask for tons of unnecessary permissions, and making it act erratic for anyone used to Fable 5.2, where you just give it a task and it gets it done. That's one of the reasons, among others, behind the initial wave of hate.

In my testing, the model is incredible, fast and absurdly cheap, especially in Three.js, Blender and similar tools. More posts soon.

Bottom line: its first output is already better than what I'm used to from Fable 5.2, and it lets me iterate 2 to 4x more before hitting what Fable would cost. Speaking strictly about 3D and game dev, of course.

2026-11-18 10:05 北京时间 ｜ Source: @TokenGremlin ｜ 原帖链接

GPT-6.2 Astra 非常擅长利用你提示词中的模糊之处为己所用。

例如，如果你让它“构建一个 X 的 3D 模型”，它会从网上抓一个现成素材，当作自己的作品呈现出来。除非你告诉它“不要使用外部素材”。

以前的模型不是这样的。这个模型“狡猾地聪明”。它能交付很好的结果，但总在找捷径以更快完成任务，而且用的 token 非常少。

原文：GPT-6.2 Astra is really good at exploiting ambiguity in your prompts and using it to its advantage.

For example, if you ask it to "build a 3D model of X," it'll grab a ready-made asset from the internet and present it as its own creation. Unless you tell it "don't use external assets."

Previous models weren't like this. This one is sneaky smart. It delivers great results, but it's always looking for shortcuts to finish the task faster, and it uses very few tokens.

2026-11-18 23:57 北京时间 ｜ Source: @TokenGremlin ｜ 原帖链接

脚本，百分之百。Blender 有 Python API（bpy），所以模型可以直接用代码建 mesh、材质和灯光。更快、更精确，token 也便宜得多。

Computer use 很适合在视觉上检查结果，或去碰只有 UI 才暴露的功能；但要生成资产，还是脚本赢。

原文：Scripts, 100%. Blender has a Python API (bpy), so the model can build meshes, materials and lighting straight from code. Way faster, more precise and much cheaper on tokens.

Computer use is great for checking the result visually or poking at stuff only the UI exposes, but for generating assets, scripts win.所回复原帖（@_voltcrash）：@TokenGremlin @TokenGremlin I have question

The assets in blender should get generated using scripts or via computer use?

> 跨 session 的上下文默认会被调出。团队现在会为不同项目创建专门的 bot；这些不同的 Devin 在必要时可以互相沟通，但各自有独立的记忆库和目标。每个 bot 现在都有自己长期存在的身份。从"Devin 是一群短暂存在的 agent"转变为"Devin 是一组 bot，每个都有自己持久的身份和记忆"，正在改变人们使用和 prompt Devin 的方式。

Each bot now has its own, long lived identity. The transition from "Devin is a swarm of ephemeral agents" to "Devin is a collection of bots, each with their own persistent identity and memory" is changing how people use and prompt Devin.

@pennykoi（保留 7 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 8 条）

2026-11-19 01:54 北京时间 ｜ Source: @pennykoi ｜ 原帖链接

所有人都在看 Anthropic 明天的 IPO。我看的是 OpenAI。

关于股价会在哪里交易，讨论已经很多。我更关心 Anthropic 的数字对 OpenAI 意味着什么。Anthropic 增长更快、运营效率高得多，而且即将在公开市场融资。

根据与实验室人士的交流和对行业的跟踪，以下是我认为 Anthropic 上市后 OpenAI 将面临更大压力的原因。

1. 据其修订后的 S-1，Anthropic 在 10 月底的 run-rate 约达 810 亿美元，仅 10 月就增加了大约 100 亿美元。我听说这高于其自身指引。11 月下旬和 12 月应会受美国假期明显冲击。所以这意味着四季度可能好于此前担忧。

2. 买方对这次 IPO 情绪很热，有两个原因：Jensen 的问题意味着来自中国开源的威胁减弱，价格压力变小；而 OpenAI 难以拿出一款 Fable 级别的模型来回应。

3. S-1 里最大的意外是 Anthropic 运营得有多精瘦。推理只占其算力的一小部分，收入成本非常低。业内粗略基准是 1GW 算力大约对应 500 亿美元 ARR，而 Anthropic 的 1GW 刚刚够用。这是实打实的毛利率优势。

4. OpenAI 相对 Anthropic 有三道缺口：基础设施效率、财务效率（利润率和亏损），以及一旦 Anthropic 上市之后的资本成本。

5. OpenAI 有两个内置的利润率问题。它每年仍要花数十亿美元服务免费用户。收入也严重偏向订阅，其利润率远低于 API。而且因为 Anthropic 对封禁很严，大量重度消费用户最终去了 OpenAI。

6. Astra 6.2 的发布在网上被普遍差评。人们抱怨令人不适的回复和糟糕的设计品味。它给人匆忙之感，存在 post-training 和对齐问题。

7. 据报 OpenAI 的 ARR 在 500 亿美元出头，可能达不到年底目标。在前沿实验室中，每月净新增看起来正从大约 100–150 亿美元放缓到 70–80 亿美元。这对 AI 交易不是好事。

8. 明年看起来会很难受。只要还有现金，OpenAI 就不能按比 Anthropic 更少的算力来规划，因为在算力上落后就意味着退出竞赛。这意味着在更小、质量更低的收入上承担相近的算力账单，还要养活庞大的免费用户。二级市场空头把明年亏损放在 700–800 亿美元，并认为 IPO 是成败关键。如果到三四月 OpenAI 仍落后半代，而且每次发布都被 Anthropic 更好的新模型压过，IPO 出现 down round 是真实风险。

9. 递归自我改进（RSI）直接决定实验室研发推进的速度。OpenAI 的 RSI 很强，但我的感觉是 Anthropic 仍领先。谁的 RSI 更强，谁就可能先到达奇点。

10. 反过来，一个如此强大的 Anthropic 对整个 AI 供应链未必是好事。当一个买方拥有这么大的议价权，所有卖给它的人都会感到挤压。据报它与 xAI/SpaceX 的续约谈判因定价破裂，它可能也想和 Microsoft 重新谈判。

很想听听别人怎么看 OpenAI 接下来六个月。（附图/图表）

原文：Everyone's watching Anthropic's IPO tomorrow. I'm watching OpenAI.

There's plenty of talk about where the stock will trade. I'm more interested in what Anthropic's numbers mean for OpenAI. Anthropic is growing faster, running much more efficiently, and about to raise money in the public markets.

From talking to people at the labs and tracking the industry, here's why I think OpenAI will be under even more pressure once Anthropic is public.

1. Anthropic's run-rate hit about $81B at the end of October, per its amended S-1, up roughly $10B in October alone. I'm hearing that's ahead of its own guidance. Late November and December should take a real hit from US holidays. So it means Q4 may come in better than feared.

2. Buyside sentiment into the IPO is hot for two reasons. The Jensen problem means less threat from Chinese open source, so less pricing pressure. And OpenAI is struggling to respond with a fable level model.

3. The biggest surprise in the S-1 is how lean Anthropic runs. Inference is a small share of its compute and cost of revenue is very low. The rough industry benchmark is 1GW of compute for about $50B of ARR, and Anthropic's 1GW is just enough. That's a real gross margin edge.

4. OpenAI has three gaps with Anthropic, in infra efficiency, financial efficiency (margins and losses) and, once Anthropic is public, cost of capital.

5. OpenAI has two built-in margin problems. It still spends billions a year serving free users. Its revenue also leans heavily on subscriptions, which earn much lower margins than API. And since Anthropic is strict about bans, a lot of heavy consumer users end up on OpenAI.

6. The Astra 6.2 launch got panned across the internet. People complained about off-putting responses and bad design taste. It felt rushed, with post-training and alignment issues.

7. OpenAI's ARR is reportedly in the low $50Bs, likely short of its year-end target. Across the frontier labs, monthly net adds look to be slowing from roughly $10-15B to $7-8B . Not great for the AI trade.

8. Next year looks rough. As long as it has cash, OpenAI can't plan for less compute than Anthropic, because falling behind on compute means falling out of the race. That means a similar compute bill on smaller, lower-quality revenue, plus a huge free user base to feed. Bears in the secondary market put next year's loss at $70-80B and see the IPO as make-or-break. If OpenAI is still half a generation behind by March or April, with every launch topped by a better one from Anthropic, a down round at IPO is a real risk.

9. Recursive self-improvement (RSI) directly drives how fast a lab's R&D moves. OpenAI's RSI is strong, but my sense is Anthropic's is still ahead. Whoever has the stronger RSI could be the first to reach the singularity.

10. The flip side is that an Anthropic this powerful isn't necessarily good for the AI supply chain as a whole. When one buyer has this much leverage, everyone selling to it feels the squeeze. Its renewal talks with xAI/SpaceX reportedly broke down over pricing, and it may want to renegotiate with Microsoft too.

Curious how others see OpenAI's next six months.

AI 正在加速漏洞发现，但匆忙打补丁现在正在造成宕机。瓶颈正从“找到漏洞”转向“在不中断生产的情况下修复它们”。

这对网络安全整体是正面的。AI 可能会让代码扫描和补丁生成商品化，但这些并不是大多数大型安全平台的核心收入驱动因素。

$CRWD、$PANW、$NET 和 $ZS 更有位置通过其遥测、执行和基础设施能力，从上升的安全复杂度中受益。

$QLYS 和 $TENB 对漏洞管理有更直接的敞口，既有机会也有竞争风险。

真正的价值正转向安全的修复、生产级执行和回滚——而不仅仅是发现更多漏洞。

（引用：华尔街高管和 NSA 告知国会，宕机是由修补 AI 模型识别出的漏洞所引起；AI 并未直接造成宕机，而是发现了太多问题，在攻击前匆忙修复压垮了系统。）

原文：AI is accelerating vulnerability discovery, but the rush to patch is now causing outages. The bottleneck is shifting from finding vulnerabilities to fixing them without disrupting production.

This is largely positive for cybersecurity. AI may commoditize code scanning and patch generation, but these are not core revenue drivers for most major security platforms.

$CRWD, $PANW, $NET and $ZS are better positioned to benefit from rising security complexity through their telemetry, enforcement and infrastructure capabilities.

$QLYS and $TENB have more direct exposure to vulnerability management, creating both opportunities and competitive risks.

The real value is shifting toward safe remediation, production-level enforcement and rollback—not simply finding more vulnerabilities.引用原帖（@jstein_sun）：NEWS: Wall Street execs and NSA have told Congress that outages were caused by patching vulnerabilities identified by AI models, sources say

原文：@MoFundaAI shared on the NVentures Stage how Funda is working towards solving Finance's last mile.
